珍酒李渡集團一直是富有溫度和社會責任感的企           Our Group always acts with warmth and a strong sense of social
業。我們一直以為我們的消費者釀造高品質白            responsibility. We are dedicated to brewing high-quality baijiu
酒，為我們的合作夥伴和員工以及他們背後的家           for our consumers and creating a better life for our partners,
庭創造更好的生活為己任。我們十分注重在企業           employees, and their families. We prioritize giving back to
發展過程中回報社會，為地區經濟作出貢獻。            society and contributing to the regional economy throughout
2024年，珍酒 榮登貴州民營白酒企業納稅榜首。        our corporate development. In 2024, Zhen Jiu topped the list of
從2009 年收購珍酒 至2024 年，珍酒 創造的就業    private baijiu companies in Guizhou Province in terms of tax
崗位增加 40 倍，繳稅金額增長 340 倍。2024 年   contributions. Since acquiring Zhen Jiu in 2009 until 2024, there
度，集團取得了 AA 的萬得 ESG 評分，在白酒行      has been a 40-fold increase in the number of jobs created by
業上市企業中名列首位。2025 年，籍助珍酒 成        Zhen Jiu , and a 340-fold increase in Zhen Jiu’s tax contributions.
立50週年的契機，我們將在各地廣泛開展慈善活          In 2024, we received an ESG “AA” rating from Wind, ranking
動，發動全體員工投身當地扶貧工作，以實際行           among the top of all listed baijiu companies. In 2025, to mark
動踐行社會責任。                        and celebrate Zhen Jiu’s 50th anniversary, we will widely
                                carry out charitable activities in various regions, mobilizing all
                                employees to participate in local poverty alleviation efforts,

2025 年對於珍酒李渡集團來說是極具里程碑意         The year 2025 is a landmark year for our Group, as it marks the
義的一年。2025 年是珍酒 成立的 50 週年，從珍     50th anniversary of Zhen Jiu . From the 50-year journey of Zhen
酒 傳承50載的風雨歷程，到李渡 跨越上千年的歷        Jiu ’s heritage to Li Du ’s thousand-year historical culture, we
史文化，我們珍視每一份傳統工藝的精髓，堅守           cherish every essence of traditional craftsmanship and uphold

一流釀酒企業邁進」願景的元年，我們每個珍酒           year for establishing the vision of “striving to be a world-class

珍酒李渡集團有限公司 ZJLD GROUP INC                                                 年度報告 ANNUAL REPORT 2024
        18                                                    管理層討論及分析
                     Management Discussion And Analysis


 李渡 的創立乃為了致敬其起源地江西省李渡鎮 －                   Li Du was established to honor its origin from Lidu, Jiangxi
 一座有著悠久白酒釀造傳統的中國古鎮。李渡 白                    Province, an ancient town in China that is steeped in its long-
 酒釀造工藝被認定為江西省非物質文化遺產。於                     standing tradition of making baijiu. The baijiu-making techniques
 2002年，我們於江西省李渡鎮翻新生產基地時發                   of Li Du were recognized as an Intangible Cultural Heritage of
 現了一個元朝（公元1271年至公元1368年）的酒                 Jiangxi. In 2002, an ancient baijiu distillery of the Yuan Dynasty
 窖，該酒窖隨後被列入全國重點文物保護單位名                     (1271 AD to 1368 AD) was discovered during the renovation
 單。於2023年4月27日，李渡 與其他六家領先的                 of our production facility in Lidu, Jiangxi Province and was
 白酒企業共同申請將中國白酒列入中國世界文化                     subsequently named on the list of Major National Historical and
 遺產暫定目錄。李渡 於2024年獲得重大認可，被                  Cultural Sites. On April 27, 2023, Li Du , together with six other
 評為第八批國家重點農業產業化龍頭企業，並榮                     leading baijiu companies, jointly applied for Chinese baijiu to
 獲中華人民共和國商務部授予的「中華老字號」稱                    be included in China’s tentative list of world cultural heritage.

                                           producing porcelain. The Hongzhou Kiln discovered in Li Du
                                           distillery and the ancient baijiu distillery of the Yuan Dynasty

                                           and cultural heritage.

銷售及經銷開支                           Selling and Distribution Expenses
我們的銷售及經銷開支包括(i)與我們在不同媒體           Our selling and distribution expenses consist of (i) advertisement
渠道（如電視及電台、機場及火車站以及線上渠             expenses relating to our advertisements placed across different
道）投放廣告以及我們其他線上及線下營銷及推             media channels, such as television and radio, airport and
廣活動有關的廣告開支；(ii)員工薪酬，包括我們          railway stations and online channels, as well as relating to our
的銷售及經銷人員的薪金、福利及花紅；(iii) 我         other online and offline marketing and promotion activities, (ii)
們的銷售及經銷人員產生的差旅及辦公開支；及             employee compensation, including salaries, welfare and bonuses
(iv)其他，例如分配給銷售及經銷活動的折舊及攤          for our sales and distribution personnel, (iii) travel and office
銷。                                expenses incurred by our sales and distribution personnel, and
                                  (iv) others, such as depreciation and amortization allocated to

  時採取適當行動。                      To enhance overall risk management, the Group will continue

Management Discussion And Analysis


首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃於2023年4月11日      The Post-IPO Equity Incentive Plan was approved by way of
經股東決議案獲批准，並自上市日期起生效。該         shareholders’ resolution on April 11, 2023, with effect from
計劃的目的是激勵合資格參與者並就彼等對本集         the Listing Date. The purpose of the plan is to incentivize and
團作出的貢獻給予獎勵，使彼等的利益與本公司         reward eligible participants for their contribution to the Group
利益一致，藉此鼓勵彼等努力提升本公司價值。         and to align their interests with that of the Company, so as to
首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃的主要條款在招股         encourage them to work towards enhancing the value of the

與僱員、客戶及供應商的主要關係             KEY RELATIONSHIPS WITH EMPLOYEES,
                            CUSTOMERS AND SUPPLIERS
我們的成功取決於我們吸引、挽留及激勵合資格       Our success depends on our ability to attract, retain and
僱員的能力。我們主要通過校園招聘會及線上渠       motivate qualified employees. We recruit employees primarily
道（包括我們的公司網站及第三方招聘網站）招聘      through on-campus job fairs and online channels, including our

首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃               POST-IPO EQUITY INCENTIVE PLAN
首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃於2023年4月11日    The Post-IPO Equity Incentive Plan was approved by way of
經股東決議案獲批准，並自上市日期起生效。該       shareholders’ resolution on April 11, 2023, with effect from
計劃的目的是激勵合資格參與者並就彼等對本集       the Listing Date. The purpose of this plan is to incentivize and
團作出的貢獻給予獎勵，使彼等的利益與本公司       reward eligible participants for their contribution to the Group
利益一致，藉此鼓勵彼等努力提升本公司價值。       and to align their interests with that of the Company, so as to
                            encourage them to work towards enhancing the value of the
                            Company.

首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃的主要條款概述如       A summary of the principal terms of the Post-IPO Equity
下：                          Incentive Plan are summarized as follows:

(a)   目的                    (a)   Purpose
      首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃的目的是激          The purpose of the Post-IPO Equity Incentive Plan is
      勵合資格參與者（定義見下文）並就彼等對         to incentivize and reward the Eligible Participants (as
      本集團作出的貢獻給予獎勵，使彼等的利          defined below) for their contribution to the Group and
      益與本公司利益一致，藉此鼓勵彼等努力          to align their interests with that of our Company so as to
      提升本公司價值。                    encourage them to work towards enhancing the value of
                                  our Company.

  企業管治常規                        CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES
  本公司深知企業管治是卓越運營的基石。我們不         The Company understands that corporate governance is
  斷完善企業管治架構，高度重視內外部企業風險         the cornerstone of excellent operations. We have been
  管理及合規工作。我們亦一直致力於加強董事          continuously refining our corporate governance structure,
  會層面的ESG工作，同時密切監察持份者對我們        placing high importance on enterprise risk management and
  ESG工作的期望及要求。展望未來，我們的目標        compliance both internally and externally. We have also been
  是不斷優化我們的管治體系，提高管治水平，滿         persistently working on enhancing ESG efforts at board level,
  足監管要求，並營造一個透明、多元化和值得信         while closely monitoring the expectations and demands of
  賴的企業環境。                       stakeholders regarding our ESG work. Looking forward, we
                                aim to continuously optimize our governance system, enhance
                                governance standards, meet regulatory requirements, and foster
                                a transparent, diverse and trustworthy corporate environment.

  本公司的企業管治常規以企業管治守則所載的原         The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on the
  則及守則條文為基礎，且本公司已採納企業管治         principles and code provisions as set out in the CG Code and
  守則的守則條文作為其本身的企業管治守則。董         the Company has adopted the code provisions of the CG Code
  事會認為，於報告期內，本公司已遵守企業管治         as its own code of corporate governance. The Board is of the
  守則所載的所有守則條文。董事會將定期檢討及         view that the Company has complied with all code provisions as
  加強其企業管治常規，以確保本公司繼續符合企         set out in the CG Code during the Reporting Period. The Board
  業管治守則的規定。                     will periodically review and enhance its corporate governance
                                practices to ensure that the Company continues to meet the
                                requirements of the CG Code.

主席及行政總裁                     CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER
企業管治守則的守則條文第 C.2.1 條規定，主席   Code Provision C.2.1 of the CG Code stipulates that the roles
與行政總裁的職位應有區分，不應由同一人擔        of chairman and chief executive should be separate and should
任。主席與行政總裁之職責分工應清晰界定並以       not be performed by the same individual. The division of
書面形式列載。                     responsibilities between the chairman and chief executive should
                            be clearly established and set out in writing.

於報告期內，本公司主席及行政總裁分別由吳向       During the Reporting Period, the chairman and chief executive
東先生及顏先生擔任。吳向東先生作為本公司主       officer of the Company are held by Mr. Wu and Mr. Yan,
席，負責本集團的整體戰略規劃及業務方向，而       respectively. Mr. Wu, as the chairman of the Company, is
顏先生作為本公司行政總裁，負責本集團的整體       responsible for the overall strategic planning and business
管理及業務營運。                    direction of our Group, while Mr. Yan, as the chief executive
                            officer of the Company, is responsible for the overall
                            management and business operation of our Group.

董事會將不時檢討企業管治架構及常規，並於董       The Board will review the corporate governance structure
事會認為適當時作出必要安排。              and practices from time to time and shall make necessary
                            arrangements when the Board considers appropriate.

要。為確保董事會獲得獨立意見及投入，本公司       to good corporate governance. To ensure independent views and
已在本公司企業管治框架下設立有效機制。提名       input are available to the Board, the Company has put in place
委員會的成員大多數為獨立非執行董事，負責評       effective mechanisms in the Company’s corporate governance
估獨立非執行董事候選人是否合適和獨立，並每       framework. The Nomination Committee, comprising a majority
年對各獨立非執行董事的獨立性進行審查。獨立       of independent non-executive Directors, assesses the suitability
非執行董事至少每年一次在其他董事不在場的情       and independence of potential candidates to be appointed
況下與主席開會，並可透過正式和非正式的渠        as independent non-executive directors and conducts annual
道，與管理層及包括主席在內的其他董事交流意       review of the independence of each independent non-executive
見。全體董事在有需要時亦可獲取獨立的專業意       director. The independent non-executive Directors meet with
見。有關機制每年進行檢討，以確保成效。於報       the Chairman at least once annually without the presence
告期內，董事會已審查該等機制的實施及成效，       of other Directors, and they can interact with management
結果令人滿意。                     and other Directors including the Chairman through formal
                            and informal means. Independent professional advice is also

  但是不少於三分之一的人數）將輪值告退，但前       of three, then the number nearest to, but not less than, one-third,
  提是每一位董事（包括有特定任期的董事）須最少      shall retire from office by rotation, provided that every Director
  每三年輪值告退一次。退任的董事將任職至其退       (including those appointed for a specific term) shall be subject to

會議議程及相關董事會文件將至少在擬舉行董事       The agenda and accompanying Board papers are dispatched at
會會議或董事委員會會議的三天前送出，以確保       least three days before the intended Board meetings or Board
董事有充足時間審閱有關文件及充分準備出席會       committee meetings to ensure that the Directors have sufficient
議。倘董事或委員會成員未能出席會議，則彼等       time to review the papers and be adequately prepared for the
將被告知將予討論的事宜及於會議召開前有機會       meetings. When Directors or committee members are unable
知會主席有關彼等的意見。                to attend a meeting, they will be advised of the matters to be
                            discussed and given an opportunity to make their views known
                            to the chairman prior to the meeting.

間公開供查閱。各董事會會議及董事委員會會議       for inspection at any reasonable time on reasonable notice
的會議記錄已詳細記錄董事會及董事委員會所考       by all Directors. Minutes of the Board meetings and Board
慮的事項及所達致的決定，包括董事提出的任何       committee meetings are recorded in sufficient detail on the
問題或表達的不同意見。該等會議記錄的初稿及       matters considered by the Board and the Board committees and
定稿已於並將於舉行董事會會議後的合理時間內       the decisions reached, including any concerns raised by the
分別送交全體董事，以供彼等提出意見及作記        Directors or dissenting views expressed. Draft and final versions
錄。                          of these meeting minutes have been and will be sent to all
                            Directors for their comments and records respectively, within a
                            reasonable time after the Board meeting is held.

及技能。此舉旨在確保彼等對董事會作出知情及       to develop and refresh their knowledge and skills. This is to
相關貢獻。                       ensure that their contribution to the board remains informed and
                            relevant.

本公司亦不時更新全體董事有關上市規則及企業       professional development program for Directors. The Company
管治常規的任何重大變動。此外，每名新委任的       has also updated all Directors on any material changes in the

政策。                         an induction package covering the statutory and regulatory

  審計委員會的主要職責包括（惟不限於）就本集團           The primary duties of the Audit Committee include, without

  股息政策                             DIVIDEND POLICY
  我們已採納向股東宣派、支付或分派利潤作為股            We have adopted a dividend policy in relation to the
  息的股息政策。支付股息須遵守適用的法律法             declaration, payment or distribution of its profits as dividends
  規，包括開曼群島法律及組織章程細則。               to the Shareholders. The payment of dividends is subject to
                                   compliance with applicable laws and regulations including the
                                   laws of the Cayman Islands and the Articles of Association.

  根據股息政策，我們宣派及支付的任何股息將由            According to the dividend policy, our Board has the absolute
  董事會全權酌情決定，考慮的因素包括我們的實            discretion to determine any declaration and payment of
  際及預期經營業績、現金流量及財務狀況、整             dividend, taking into account factors including our actual and
  體業務狀況及業務戰略、預期營運資金要求及未            expected results of operations, cash flow and financial position,
  來擴張計劃、法律、監管及其他合同限制，以及            general business conditions and business strategies, expected
  董事會認為合適的其他因素。根據組織章程細則            working capital requirements and future expansion plans, legal,
  第133 條，本公司股東大會可不時向股東派發股          regulatory and other contractual restrictions and any other

風險管理及內部控制                   RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL
                            CONTROL
董事會整體及共同負責確保有效的風險管理及內       Our Board has the overall and collective responsibility to ensure
部控制機制，並定期審閱其於保障本集團資產及       an effective risk management and internal control mechanism
股東權益方面的有效性。                 with periodic review on its effectiveness in safeguarding our
                            Group’s assets and Shareholders’ interests.

我們的風險管理及內部控制系統旨在管理而非消       Our risk management and internal control systems is designed to
除未能達致業務目標的風險，並僅就重大錯誤陳       manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure so as to achieve
述或損失提供合理而非絕對的保證。            our business objectives, and to provide only a reasonable but
                            not an absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.

根據企業管治守則的守則條文第 D.2.2 條的規    In accordance with the requirements of Code Provision D.2.2
定，本公司已建立健全的風險管理及內部控制系       of the CG Code, the Company has established a sound risk
統，以確保本公司擁有充足的資源、合資格及經       management and internal control system to ensure that the
驗豐富的員工、培訓課程及預算用於其內部審        Company has sufficient resources, qualified and experienced
計、財務報告職能以及與本公司ESG表現及報告      staffs, training courses and budget for its internal audit, financial
有關的職能。                      reporting functions as well as those relating to the Company’s
                            ESG performance and reporting.

識別、評估及管理重大風險的程序             Procedures on Identifying, Evaluating and Managing
                            Significant Risks
我們將每年進行一次企業風險評估，以識別我們       Enterprise risk assessment will be conducted annually to identify
經營業務範圍的現有及潛在風險，包括（惟不限       the current and potential risks in the area of businesses where
於）戰略、市場、財務、營運、合規、ESG以及氣     we operate, including but not limited to the risks arising from
候相關事宜產生的風險。董事會將持續評估或委       strategic, market, financial, operational, compliance, ESG and
聘合資格獨立第三方以評估經營風險及審閱本集       climate-related matters. Our Board will continuously assess
團現有的風險管理策略、指標及目標，以及內部       or engage qualified independent third parties to evaluate the
控制系統，並將納入並實施任何改善措施以管理       risks in our operations and to review our Group’s existing risk
及減輕已識別的有關風險。                management strategy, metrics and targets as well as internal
                            control systems. Any improvement measures will be incorporated
                            and implemented to manage and mitigate such risks identified.

                             1    公司資料                              1   CORPORATE INFORMATION
                                  珍 酒 李 渡 集 團 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」）於       ZJLD Group Inc (the “Company”) was incorporated in the
                                  2021 年9 月24 日根據開曼群島公司法（經              Cayman Islands as an exempted company with limited
                                  修訂）於開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限                    liability under the Companies Act (as revised) of the
                                  公司。本公司股份於2023年4月27日在香                 Cayman Islands on 24 September 2021. The Company’s
                                  港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上                  shares were listed on the Main Board of The Stock
                                  市。本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）                  Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”)
                                  主要從事釀造、生產及銷售白酒產品。                     on 27 April 2023. The Company and its subsidiaries
                                                                        (together, the “Group”) are principally engaged in the
                                                                        making, production and sales of baijiu products.

               多項其他因素而作出，其結果構成                             on historical experience and various other factors

        (d)    附屬公司                                  (d)   Subsidiaries
               附屬公司指本集團控制的實體。當                             Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group.
               本集團就參與該實體業務而承擔可                             The Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or
               變回報風險或享有可變回報權利以                             has rights to, variable returns from its involvement
               及能透過對該實體行使權力影響該                             with the entity and has the ability to affect those
               等回報時，本集團控制該實體。附                             returns through its power over the entity. The
               屬公司的財務報表自控制權開始之                             financial statements of subsidiaries are included in
               日起納入綜合財務報表，直至控制                             the consolidated financial statements from the date
               權終止之日止。                                     on which control commences until the date on
                                                           which control ceases.

               集團內公司間結餘、交易及集團內                             Intra-group balances and transactions, and any
               公司間交易產生的任何未變現收入                             unrealised income and expenses (except for foreign
               及開支（外幣交易收益或虧損除外）                            currency transaction gains or losses) arising from
               均予以抵銷。集團內公司間交易所                             intra-group transactions, are eliminated. Unrealised
               引致未變現虧損的抵銷方法與未變                             losses resulting from intra-group transactions are




                                                                                                                     年度報告 ANNUAL REPORT 2024
               現收益相同，但抵銷額只限於沒有                             eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains,
               證據顯示已出現減值的部分。                               but only to the extent that there is no evidence of
                                                           impairment.

               本集團於附屬公司的權益變動，倘                             Changes in the Group’s interests in a subsidiary
               不會導致喪失控制權，便會按權益                             that do not result in a loss of control are accounted
               交易列賬。                                       for as equity transactions.

               當本集團喪失對附屬公司的控制權                             When the Group loses control of a subsidiary,
               時，其終止確認附屬公司的資產及                             it derecognises the assets and liabilities of the
               負債，以及任何相關非控股權益及                             subsidiary, and any related non-controlling




                                                                                                                     珍酒李渡集團有限公司 ZJLD GROUP INC
               權益的其他組成部分。由此產生的                             interests and other components of equity. Any
               任何收益或虧損在損益中確認。於                             resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.
               該前附屬公司所保留的任何權益於                             Any interest retained in that former subsidiary is
               失去控制權時按公允價值計量。                              measured at fair value when control is lost.

               除非投資分類為持作出售，否則本                             In the Company’s statement of financial position,
               公司財務狀況表內於附屬公司的投                             an investment in a subsidiary is stated at cost less
               資按成本減去減值虧損（見附註2(g)                          impairment losses (see Note 2(g)(ii)), unless it is
               (ii)）後入賬。                                   classified as held for sale.

                                  (e)   物業、廠房及設備                           (e)   Property, plant and equipment
                                        物業、廠房及設備按成本（包括資                          Property, plant and equipment are stated at cost,
                                        本 化 借 款 成 本 ）減 累 計 折 舊 及 任               which includes capitalised borrowing costs, less
                                        何累計減值虧損入賬（見附註 2( g )                     accumulated depreciation and any accumulated
                                        (ii)）。                                   impairment losses (see Notes 2(g)(ii)).

                                        倘物業、廠房及設備項目的重要部                          If significant parts of an item of property, plant and

                                        責任而導致本集團產生財務虧損的               default on its contractual obligations resulting in
                                        風險。本集團的信貸風險主要來自               a financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit
                                        貿易應收款項。本公司董事認為，               risk is primarily attributable to trade receivables.
                                        由於對手方為信譽良好的銀行及本               The directors of the Company are of the opinion
                                        集團認為彼等的信貸風險較低，因               that the Group’s exposure to credit risks arising
                                        此本集團因銀行及手頭現金、應收               from cash at bank and on hand, bills receivables
                                        票據及應收利息（計入預付款項、               and interest receivables (included in prepayments,
                                        按金及其他應收款項）面臨的信貸               deposits and other receivables) is limited because
                                        風險有限。本集團並無提供將令本               the counterparties are banks with good credit
                                        集團面臨信貸風險的任何擔保。                standing, for which the Group considers to have
                                                                      low credit risk. The Group does not provide any
                                                                      guarantees which would expose the Group to
                                                                      credit risk.
